<!-- 由 scripts/gen-rust-notices.py 產生，不要手改。 -->

# Rust 依賴授權

安裝包裡的 DLL 與執行檔是靜態連結 Rust 相依後的產物，散布時要一併附上這些
相依的授權聲明。資料、模型與題庫的授權另外列在
[`THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md`](THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md)，本專案自己的授權見
[`../LICENSE`](../LICENSE)（AGPL-3.0-only）。

範圍：從 `Cargo.lock` 解析 `jc-tsf`、`jc-engine`、`jc-cli`、`jc-settings-ui`、
`jc-tsf-smoke` 的**正常依賴**，聯集
`x86_64-pc-windows-msvc`
`i686-pc-windows-msvc`
`x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu`
三個目標平台。dev-dependency、build-dependency 與 proc-macro crate 不會進
安裝包，因此不列。

共 617 個 crate、337 份不同的授權全文。

上架包沒有附授權檔的 crate 只列 Cargo.toml 的宣告；條款本身不含著作權人時
（Apache-2.0、Unicode-3.0），改為引用同樣條款的其他 crate 的全文。

重新產生：`python3 scripts/gen-rust-notices.py`；CI 用 `--check` 比對，
改了 `Cargo.toml`／`Cargo.lock` 卻沒重跑會讓 build 失敗。

## 授權全文

同一份全文只列一次，因此多數 crate 共用同一個章節。標題取自全文的第一行。
全文只做排版正規化（CRLF→LF、去行尾空白、去頭尾空行），條文內容不動。

```text
*ring* uses an "ISC" license, like BoringSSL used to use, for new code
files. See LICENSE-other-bits for the text of that license.

See LICENSE-BoringSSL for code that was sourced from BoringSSL under the
Apache 2.0 license. Some code that was sourced from BoringSSL under the ISC
license. In each case, the license info is at the top of the file.

See src/polyfill/once_cell/LICENSE-APACHE and src/polyfill/once_cell/LICENSE-MIT
for the license to code that was sourced from the once_cell project.
```

```text
Recipient's use, modification, or sharing of any portions of the
Data that are in the public domain or that may be used, modified,
or shared under any other legal exception or limitation.

## 2. Conditions for Sharing Data

long as the Data Recipient makes available the text of this agreement
with the shared Data.

## 3. No Restrictions on Results

with respect to the use, modification, or sharing of Results.

## 4. No Warranty; Limitation of Liability

4.1. All Data Recipients receive the Data subject to the following
terms:

THE DATA IS PROVIDED ON AN "AS IS" BASIS, WITHOUT REPRESENTATIONS,
WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, EITHER EXPRESS OR IMPLIED
INCLUDING, WITHOUT LIMITATION, ANY WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF TITLE,
NON-INFRINGEMENT, MERCHANTABILITY OR FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE.

EVEN IF ADVISED OF THE POSSIBILITY OF SUCH DAMAGES.

## 5. Definitions

this agreement.

5.2. "Data Provider" means any person who is the source of Data
provided under this agreement and in reliance on a Data Recipient's
agreement to its terms.

5.3. "Data Recipient" means any person who receives Data directly
or indirectly from a Data Provider and agrees to the terms of this
agreement.

5.4. "Results" means any outcome obtained by computational analysis
of Data, including for example machine learning models and models'
insights.
```
